# Visual atlas: layout and routing

Baseline `af70d8317797d5a522acffdd604819376a3ec893`. This is the complete numbered storyboard and rendering ledger. Every entry in [coverage.csv](coverage.csv) resolves to a family below or a justified nonvisual explanation. Open [atlas-preview.html](atlas-preview.html) for the actual SVGs, shared-scale comparisons, source, exact changed lines and editor recipes.

## Evidence contract

- All included SVG assets were generated by the pinned Draftably compiler or its own `renderSvg` function. The supplied PNG contact sheet is preserved as supplied evidence.
- Public-site interactions were checked separately; they are not evidence that the hosted version equals the pinned baseline.
- Cold routing sweeps freeze **world-space node rectangles** and use `layout root row` so compiled flow lanes do not confound route costs. Group-channel examples intentionally recompile geometry.
- Numeric low/default/high samples are teaching points within the documented domain, not claims of monotonic output. Every categorical choice is retained even when it has the same result.
- Exact geometry equality, rendered path equality, coarse vary tags, and equal headline metrics are different claims. The JSON ledgers keep them separate.
- Warnings are part of the evidence: `check --metrics` adds quality warnings beyond `inspect`/`vary` warning counts. Deliberate crossing/overflow/blocked-route examples are captioned; errors and nonfinite geometry live only in `examples/layout/repros/`.
- Editor action sequences are reproducible specifications. Their starting source/JSON is supplied. Model execution of cold/warm/pass, timeline frames and brush modes is additionally rendered, without claiming mouse/keyboard/browser persistence was exercised.

## Reproduction

Use the pinned checkout with its installed dependencies. From this artifact directory run:

```sh
# Validate all positive .draft companions and re-export their actual SVGs.
python3 run-atlas.py --repo /absolute/path/to/draftably --out /absolute/path/to/atlas-run

# One direct command, run from the repository root.
pnpm draftably inspect /absolute/path/to/this-artifact/examples/routing/crossing-penalty-1200.draft --svg

# Use the existing screenshot command when its Chromium runtime is installed.
pnpm shoot /absolute/path/to/this-artifact/examples/engines/compound-flow.draft tmp/compound-flow.png
```

The bundled sources are complete documents, not fragments. CLI `vary` additionally requires Chrome for its mandatory PNG export in this baseline. An early attempt failed at that step; after obtaining stable Chrome, the full radius sweep completed, including PNGs and variants.json. Its outputs are retained in `evidence/cli-vary-radius/`. Direct SVG production succeeds without Chrome.

## Editor setup used by every action recipe

**F: source-backed file.** Open the exact fixture with `pnpm draftably open FILE --no-open` and use its printed Viewer URL. Playground → Editor is also source-backed. **J: native document.** Import the corresponding `examples/native-json/*.json` in a fresh plain `/app/` session without a file/example query or source hash. Otherwise Save diagram from F, start a fresh native session and import the JSON. Reset the starting document before every independent sample. Use light mode and a recorded 1440×1000 viewport except the explicit viewport experiment. Hold world scale constant; changes to display fit are not layout changes.

## 001. VL01 — Ten file engines on one unchanged graph

**Controls:** L007, L008, L009, L010, L011, L012, L013, L014, L015, L016. **Context:** cold source recompilation.

**Status:** executed: actual CLI geometry and SVG for all ten.

**Keep fixed:** The exact supplied seven-node, three-group graph; same declarations, labels, edge order, sizes, routing defaults, compile context and title. Only engine= changes.

**Teach / intended observation:** All ten reproduced metrics match the supplied evidence. Boundary alignment and compound spring remove a crossing at 81.6% and 91.4% larger frame area. Layered and compound-flow have equal canonical geometry; tidy and flow have equal dimensions but different routes.

## 028. VL28 — Ordinary cells and column counts

**Controls:** L006, L065. **Context:** cold source recompilation.

**Status:** executed: actual CLI geometry, check and SVG for every source.

## 029. VL29 — Bit, byte and variable-span fields

**Controls:** L006, L066, L069. **Context:** cold source recompilation.

**Status:** executed: actual CLI geometry, check and SVG for every source.

**Keep fixed:** Within each named sweep: identifiers, graph structure, labels, input order, theme, explicit sizes and all unrelated settings. Recipe/scope galleries contain distinct subcases; read each exact source diff before comparing. Layout positions and final routes may change.

**Teach / intended observation:** One column one unit; 48px bytes vs 24px bits absent explicit width. An omitted bit column count uses 32 units per row. Compare with the explicit-eight source of the SAME unit. An omitted byte column count uses 16 units per row. Compare with the explicit-eight source of the SAME unit. The field crosses a row boundary and has multiple rendered pieces; the longest holds the label. Unlike ordinary cells, unit spans are not clamped to one row.

- **L077 — `compare ... direction=`**
  - States/range: auto | right | down
  - Default by context: auto; stored as omitted direction; auto chooses lower log-aspect mismatch, tie prefers stacked
  - Mechanism/applicability: Panel arrangement only: right side-by-side, down stacked; explicit direction overrides aspect; no node reranking
  - Persistence: Stored in .draft appearance.compare; exports compose panels; editing canvas/canonical geometry remain merged.

  - Default by context: 1.6 when omitted
  - Mechanism/applicability: Whole exported two-panel frame target when direction=auto/omitted; irrelevant with explicit right/down; never a graph-layout force
  - Persistence: Stored in .draft appearance.compare; exports compose panels; editing canvas/canonical geometry remain merged.
